# Third-Party Notices

This file aggregates third-party packages detected in the source tree and Docker images used by this repo.
License expressions are taken from SBOM metadata; `NOASSERTION` means the license could not be determined from package metadata and must be verified from upstream sources.

## High-impact non-OSI or proprietary components

- Meta Llama 3.1 model weights (via Ollama model cache) are governed by the Llama 3.1 Community License; see `ollama/models/blobs/sha256-0ba8f0e314b4264dfd19df045cde9d4c394a52474bf92ed6a3de22a4ca31a177`.
- NVIDIA CUDA components in GPU images are governed by NVIDIA proprietary license terms (SBOM shows `LicenseRef-NVIDIA-*`).
